- **tool-eval**: Verifies the tools an agent or orchestrator depends on, by re-deriving a tool evaluation's real accuracy instead of trusting a single pass/fail score. Separates a formatting miss (a correct value scored wrong) from a real failure (a wrong value scored right), recomputes the expected answer from the raw inputs rather than a stored ground truth, and surfaces the silent-wrong cases a naive exact-match scorer rubber-stamps.
